```rs
#[tokio::test]
async fn v2_probe_detects_nextgen_identity() {
    let mock = MockConsole::start_v2().await;
    let probe = client_for(mock.addr).probe().await.unwrap();
    assert!(probe.caps.install_direct, "legacy probes must still pass");
    assert!(probe.caps.api_v2, "v2 API must be detected");
    let id = probe.identity.expect("identity present");
    assert_eq!(id.name, "Remote PKG Installer NextGen");
    assert_eq!(id.version, "0.0.2-mock");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn v2_probe_on_legacy_backend_reports_no_v2() {
    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::BrokenHex).await;
    let probe = client_for(mock.addr).probe().await.unwrap();
    assert!(probe.caps.install_direct);
    assert!(!probe.caps.api_v2, "legacy backend must not claim v2");
    assert!(probe.identity.is_none());
}

/// Serve a small pkg file so the mock's prerequisite fetch succeeds; returns
/// the temp dir, the file server (must stay alive for the whole test) and
/// the URL.
async fn serve_pkg_url() -> (
    tempfile::TempDir,
    rpi_ng_core::fileserver::FileServer,
    String,
) {
    let tmp = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = tmp.path().join("game.pkg");
    std::fs::write(&pkg_path, vec![0u8; 256 * 1024]).unwrap();
    let server = rpi_ng_core::fileserver::FileServer::start(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let id = server.register(pkg_path, Some("game.pkg".into()));
    let url = server.url_for(id).unwrap();
    (tmp, server, url)
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn v2_install_and_jobs_roundtrip() {
    let mock = MockConsole::start_v2().await;
    let client = client_for(mock.addr);
    let (_tmp, _server, url) = serve_pkg_url().await;

    let resp = client.v2_install(&[url]).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(resp.status, "success");
    assert!(resp.task_id > 0);
    let job_id = resp.job_id.expect("job id present");
    assert!(job_id > 0);
    assert_eq!(resp.title.as_deref(), Some("Mock Game"));

    // The jobs list sees the job running, then completes through polls.
    let mut completed = false;
    for _ in 0..40 {
        let jobs = client.v2_jobs().await.unwrap();
        if let Some(job) = jobs.iter().find(|j| j.id == job_id) {
            assert_eq!(job.bgft_task_id, resp.task_id);
            if job.state == "completed" {
                assert_eq!(job.transferred, job.length_total);
                completed = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(10)).await;
    }
    assert!(completed, "v2 job should complete through polls");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn v2_job_op_falls_back_to_get_after_post_404() {
    let mock = MockConsole::start_v2().await;
    let client = client_for(mock.addr);
    let (_tmp, _server, url) = serve_pkg_url().await;
    let resp = client.v2_install(&[url]).await.unwrap();
    let job_id = resp.job_id.unwrap();

    // The mock 404s POST (the NextGen routing quirk); the client must fall
    // back to GET and succeed.
    client.v2_job_op(job_id, "pause").await.unwrap();
    assert!(
        mock.v2_op_posts("pause") >= 1,
        "client must have tried POST first"
    );
    let jobs = client.v2_jobs().await.unwrap();
    let job = jobs.iter().find(|j| j.id == job_id).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(job.state, "paused");

    client.v2_job_op(job_id, "resume").await.unwrap();
    let jobs = client.v2_jobs().await.unwrap();
    let job = jobs.iter().find(|j| j.id == job_id).unwrap();
    // A small file may complete on the very next poll; "no longer paused"
    // is the observable effect of the resume.
    assert_ne!(job.state, "paused");

    client.v2_job_op(job_id, "stop").await.unwrap();
    let jobs = client.v2_jobs().await.unwrap();
    let job = jobs.iter().find(|j| j.id == job_id).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(job.state, "stopped");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn v2_install_failure_message_is_surfaced() {
    let mock = MockConsole::start_v2().await;
    mock.state.lock().unwrap().fail_installs = Some(0x8099_0007);
    let client = client_for(mock.addr);
    let err = client
        .v2_install(&["http://127.0.0.1:1/x.pkg".into()])
        .await
        .unwrap_err();
    match err {
        RpiError::BackendError { code, gloss } => {
            assert_eq!(code, 0);
            assert!(
                gloss.contains("mock failure"),
                "error string must be surfaced, was: {gloss}"
            );
        }
        other => panic!("expected BackendError, got {other:?}"),
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn v2_already_installed_reports_task_id_minus_one() {
    let mock = MockConsole::start_v2().await;
    mock.state.lock().unwrap().already_installed = true;
    let client = client_for(mock.addr);
    let (_tmp, _server, url) = serve_pkg_url().await;
    let resp = client.v2_install(&[url]).await.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(resp.task_id, -1, "-1 means already installed");
    assert!(resp.job_id.is_some());
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Engine end-to-end flow
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn build_fake_pkg(dir: &std::path::Path, name: &str, content_id: &str) -> std::path::PathBuf {
    use std::io::Write;
    // Minimal SFO.
    let entries: Vec<(&str, &str)> = vec![
        ("TITLE", "Mock Game"),
        ("CONTENT_ID", content_id),
        ("CATEGORY", "gd"),
    ];
    let mut keys = Vec::new();
    let mut values = Vec::new();
    let mut index = Vec::new();
    let key_base = 0x14 + entries.len() * 0x10;
    for (k, v) in &entries {
        let key_off = keys.len();
        keys.extend_from_slice(k.as_bytes());
        keys.push(0);
        let val_off = values.len();
        values.extend_from_slice(v.as_bytes());
        values.push(0);
        index.push((
            key_off as u16,
            0x0204u16,
            (v.len() + 1) as u32,
            (v.len() + 1) as u32,
            val_off as u32,
        ));
    }
    let mut sfo = Vec::new();
    sfo.extend_from_slice(b"\0PSF");
    sfo.extend_from_slice(&1u32.to_be_bytes());
    sfo.extend_from_slice(&(key_base as u32).to_be_bytes());
    sfo.extend_from_slice(&((key_base + keys.len()) as u32).to_be_bytes());
    sfo.extend_from_slice(&(entries.len() as u32).to_be_bytes());
    for (k, f, u, m, v) in index {
        sfo.extend_from_slice(&k.to_be_bytes());
        sfo.extend_from_slice(&f.to_be_bytes());
        sfo.extend_from_slice(&u.to_be_bytes());
        sfo.extend_from_slice(&m.to_be_bytes());
        sfo.extend_from_slice(&v.to_be_bytes());
    }
    sfo.extend_from_slice(&keys);
    sfo.extend_from_slice(&values);

    let entry_table_offset = 0x2000 + sfo.len() as u32;
    let mut entry_table = Vec::new();
    entry_table.extend_from_slice(&0x1000u32.to_be_bytes());
    entry_table.extend_from_slice(&[0u8; 0x0C]);
    entry_table.extend_from_slice(&0x2000u32.to_be_bytes());
    entry_table.extend_from_slice(&(sfo.len() as u32).to_be_bytes());
    entry_table.extend_from_slice(&[0u8; 0x0C]);

    let mut header = vec![0u8; 0x2000];
    header[0..4].copy_from_slice(&[0x7F, b'P', b'K', b'G']);
    header[0x10..0x14].copy_from_slice(&1u32.to_be_bytes());
    header[0x18..0x1C].copy_from_slice(&entry_table_offset.to_be_bytes());
    header[0x40..0x40 + content_id.len()].copy_from_slice(content_id.as_bytes());
    header[0x74..0x78].copy_from_slice(&0x1Au32.to_be_bytes());

    let data_len = 512 * 1024usize;
    let path = dir.join(name);
    let mut f = std::fs::File::create(&path).unwrap();
    f.write_all(&header).unwrap();
    f.write_all(&sfo).unwrap();
    f.write_all(&entry_table).unwrap();
    let cur = 0x2000 + sfo.len() + entry_table.len();
    f.write_all(&vec![0u8; data_len.saturating_sub(cur)])
        .unwrap();
    f.sync_all().unwrap();
    path
}

/// Drain engine events until a condition on the latest snapshot holds.
async fn wait_for_snapshot(
    handle: &rpi_ng_core::engine::EngineHandle,
    mut cond: impl FnMut(&Snapshot) -> bool,
    timeout: Duration,
) -> Snapshot {
    let mut last: Option<Snapshot> = None;
    let deadline = tokio::time::Instant::now() + timeout;
    loop {
        for ev in handle.poll_events() {
            if let Event::Snapshot(s) = ev {
                last = Some(s);
            }
        }
        if let Some(s) = &last
            && cond(s)
        {
            return s.clone();
        }
        if tokio::time::Instant::now() >= deadline {
            panic!("timeout waiting for snapshot condition; last={last:?}");
        }
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(25)).await;
    }
}

/// Drain engine events until one matches (non-matching events, including
/// snapshots, are discarded).
async fn wait_for_event(
    handle: &rpi_ng_core::engine::EngineHandle,
    mut cond: impl FnMut(&Event) -> bool,
    timeout: Duration,
) -> Event {
    let deadline = tokio::time::Instant::now() + timeout;
    loop {
        for ev in handle.poll_events() {
            if cond(&ev) {
                return ev;
            }
        }
        if tokio::time::Instant::now() >= deadline {
            panic!("timeout waiting for event");
        }
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(25)).await;
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_end_to_end_flow_completes_with_evidence() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA07777_00-MOCKGAME00000000.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA07777_00-MOCKGAME00000000",
    );

    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::BrokenHex).await;
    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };

    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    // 1. Connect to the mock console.
    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    assert!(
        snap.server.is_some(),
        "file server must be running after connect"
    );

    // 2. Add the PKG.
    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![pkg_path],
        recursive: false,
    });
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.parts.iter().any(|p| p.pkg.is_some()))
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    let item = &snap.items[0];
    assert_eq!(item.state, ItemState::Queued);
    assert_eq!(
        item.parts[0].pkg.as_ref().unwrap().title.as_deref(),
        Some("Mock Game")
    );
    assert_eq!(
        item.parts[0].pkg.as_ref().unwrap().title_id.as_deref(),
        Some("CUSA07777")
    );

    // 3. Start the queue.
    handle.send(Command::Start);

    // 4. Submission -> transferring -> installing -> completed.
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Completed),
        Duration::from_secs(30),
    )
    .await;
    let item = snap
        .items
        .iter()
        .find(|i| i.state == ItemState::Completed)
        .unwrap();
    let progress = item.progress.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(progress.total, 512 * 1024);
    assert_eq!(progress.percent, 100.0);
    assert_eq!(progress.raw_error, 0);
    assert!(item.task_id.unwrap() > 0);
    assert_eq!(item.console_title.as_deref(), Some("Mock Game"));

    // 5. The mock saw a well-formed install request and fetched the real URL.
    let installs = mock.installs();
    assert_eq!(installs.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(installs[0]["type"], "direct");
    let url = installs[0]["packages"][0].as_str().unwrap();
    assert!(url.starts_with("http://"), "URL was {url}");
    let tasks = mock.tasks();
    assert_eq!(tasks.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(tasks[&item.task_id.unwrap()].total, 512 * 1024);

    // 6. Clean shutdown.
    handle.shutdown();

    // 7. Persistence: reloading the queue keeps the completed item.
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(data_dir.path().join("queue.json")).unwrap();
    let q = rpi_ng_core::queue::Queue::from_json(&text).unwrap();
    assert!(q.items().iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Completed));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_marks_failed_on_console_error() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "fail.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA08888_00-FAILPKG0000000",
    );

    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::BrokenHex).await;
    // Tiny step so the transfer takes many polls — time to poison the task
    // with a console-side error before it completes.
    mock.state.lock().unwrap().step = 4096;

    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![pkg_path],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| !s.items.is_empty(), Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);
    // Wait for the task to exist on the mock, then poison it.
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Transferring || i.state == ItemState::Installing)
        },
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    let item_id = snap.items[0].id;
    let task_id = snap.items[0].task_id.unwrap();
    {
        let mut st = mock.state.lock().unwrap();
        if let Some(t) = st.tasks.get_mut(&task_id) {
            t.error = 0x8099_0005;
        }
    }

    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.id == item_id && i.state == ItemState::Failed)
        },
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    let item = snap.items.iter().find(|i| i.id == item_id).unwrap();
    assert!(
        item.error.as_deref().unwrap().contains("0x80990005"),
        "error was {:?}",
        item.error
    );
    handle.shutdown();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Failure policy: continue vs pause the queue after a failed transfer
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Two packages, the console rejects the first install: with the Pause policy
/// the second must never be submitted, and a Retry must restart the queue.
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_failure_policy_pauses_queue_until_retry() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let first = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA0A001_00-POLICYPAUSEONE.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA0A001_00-POLICYPAUSEONE",
    );
    let second = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA0A002_00-POLICYPAUSETWO.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA0A002_00-POLICYPAUSETWO",
    );

    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::BrokenHex).await;
    mock.state.lock().unwrap().fail_installs = Some(0x8099_0007);

    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        on_failure: FailurePolicy::Pause,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![first, second],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| s.items.len() == 2, Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);

    // First item fails at submit; the queue must park instead of advancing.
    // The pause is user-visible (a Warn log) and the following snapshot shows
    // the parked queue. Collect both from one drain loop.
    let deadline = tokio::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(20);
    let mut saw_pause_log = false;
    let mut latest: Option<Snapshot> = None;
    loop {
        for ev in handle.poll_events() {
            match ev {
                Event::Log(level, ref msg) if msg.contains("Queue paused") => {
                    assert_eq!(level, rpi_ng_core::model::LogLevel::Warn);
                    saw_pause_log = true;
                }
                Event::Snapshot(s) => latest = Some(s),
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        let settled = saw_pause_log
            && latest
                .as_ref()
                .is_some_and(|s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Failed));
        if settled {
            break;
        }
        if tokio::time::Instant::now() >= deadline {
            panic!("timeout: log={saw_pause_log} snapshot={latest:?}");
        }
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(25)).await;
    }

    // Give the engine ample time to wrongly start the second item.
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(400)).await;
    for ev in handle.poll_events() {
        if let Event::Snapshot(s) = ev {
            latest = Some(s);
        }
    }
    let latest = latest.expect("a snapshot follows the failure handling");
    let failed_id = latest
        .items
        .iter()
        .find(|i| i.state == ItemState::Failed)
        .expect("the first item is Failed")
        .id;
    assert_eq!(
        latest
            .items
            .iter()
            .filter(|i| i.state == ItemState::Queued)
            .count(),
        1,
        "second item must still be queued after a failure under the Pause policy"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        mock.installs().len(),
        1,
        "only the failed install may ever reach the console"
    );

    // Clear the failure, retry the failed item: the queue restarts and both
    // packages complete.
    mock.state.lock().unwrap().fail_installs = None;
    handle.send(Command::Retry(failed_id));
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().all(|i| i.state == ItemState::Completed),
        Duration::from_secs(30),
    )
    .await;
    assert_eq!(snap.items.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(mock.installs().len(), 3, "retry + second item = 3 installs");
    handle.shutdown();
}

/// Control: with the Continue policy a failed transfer does not stop the
/// queue (this is the pre-policy behavior, now locked in as a setting).
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_failure_policy_continue_advances_queue() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let first = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA0B001_00-POLICYCONTONE.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA0B001_00-POLICYCONTONE",
    );
    let second = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA0B002_00-POLICYCONTTWO.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA0B002_00-POLICYCONTTWO",
    );

    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::BrokenHex).await;
    // Tiny step so the first transfer takes many polls — time to poison it.
    mock.state.lock().unwrap().step = 4096;

    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        on_failure: FailurePolicy::Continue,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![first, second],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| s.items.len() == 2, Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);

    // Wait for the first task, then fail it on the console side.
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Transferring),
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    let task_id = snap
        .items
        .iter()
        .find(|i| i.task_id.is_some())
        .unwrap()
        .task_id
        .unwrap();
    {
        let mut st = mock.state.lock().unwrap();
        if let Some(t) = st.tasks.get_mut(&task_id) {
            t.error = 0x8099_0005;
        }
    }

    // Continue policy: the first item fails, the second still completes.
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Failed)
                && s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Completed)
        },
        Duration::from_secs(30),
    )
    .await;
    assert_eq!(snap.items.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(mock.tasks().len(), 2, "both items were submitted");
    handle.shutdown();
}

/// A console-side error mid-transfer (legacy dialect) parks the queue under
/// the Pause policy: the second package is never submitted.
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_failure_policy_pauses_after_console_error_mid_transfer() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let first = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA0C001_00-POLICYMIDONE.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA0C001_00-POLICYMIDONE",
    );
    let second = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA0C002_00-POLICYMIDTWO.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA0C002_00-POLICYMIDTWO",
    );

    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::BrokenHex).await;
    // Tiny step so the transfer takes many polls — time to poison it.
    mock.state.lock().unwrap().step = 4096;

    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        on_failure: FailurePolicy::Pause,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![first, second],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| s.items.len() == 2, Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);

    // Wait for the task to exist, then poison it like a real console error.
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Transferring),
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    let task_id = snap
        .items
        .iter()
        .find(|i| i.task_id.is_some())
        .unwrap()
        .task_id
        .unwrap();
    {
        let mut st = mock.state.lock().unwrap();
        if let Some(t) = st.tasks.get_mut(&task_id) {
            t.error = 0x8099_0005;
        }
    }

    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Failed),
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(400)).await;
    let mut latest = snap.clone();
    for ev in handle.poll_events() {
        if let Event::Snapshot(s) = ev {
            latest = s;
        }
    }
    assert_eq!(
        latest
            .items
            .iter()
            .filter(|i| i.state == ItemState::Queued)
            .count(),
        1,
        "second item must remain queued after a mid-transfer failure (Pause)"
    );
    assert_eq!(mock.tasks().len(), 1, "only one task was ever created");
    handle.shutdown();
}

/// v2 dialect: a job that fails on the console parks the queue (Pause).
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_failure_policy_pauses_after_v2_job_failure() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let first = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA0D001_00-POLICYV2ONE.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA0D001_00-POLICYV2ONE",
    );
    let second = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA0D002_00-POLICYV2TWO.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA0D002_00-POLICYV2TWO",
    );

    let mock = MockConsole::start_v2().await;
    // Tiny step so the first job takes many polls — time to fail it before
    // it completes on its own.
    mock.state.lock().unwrap().step = 4096;

    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        on_failure: FailurePolicy::Pause,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![first, second],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| s.items.len() == 2, Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);

    // Wait for the job to exist, then fail it on the console side.
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Transferring),
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    let job_id = snap
        .items
        .iter()
        .find(|i| i.job_id.is_some())
        .unwrap()
        .job_id
        .unwrap();
    {
        let mut st = mock.state.lock().unwrap();
        if let Some(job) = st.jobs.get_mut(&job_id) {
            job.state = "failed".into();
            job.transferred = 0;
        }
    }

    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Failed),
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(400)).await;
    let mut latest = snap.clone();
    for ev in handle.poll_events() {
        if let Event::Snapshot(s) = ev {
            latest = s;
        }
    }
    assert_eq!(
        latest
            .items
            .iter()
            .filter(|i| i.state == ItemState::Queued)
            .count(),
        1,
        "second item must remain queued after a v2 job failure (Pause)"
    );
    assert_eq!(mock.installs().len(), 1, "only one install was ever sent");
    handle.shutdown();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Restart / recovery regressions
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// A queue restored from disk can hold part ids that do not line up with a
/// fresh file server's registry (the server starts counting from zero again
/// after a restart). Submission must re-register the parts and use the ids
/// the *server* handed back, or `url_for` silently fails and the queue stalls.
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_submits_restored_queue_with_unaligned_part_ids() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA09999_00-RESTOREDPKG00000.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA09999_00-RESTOREDPKG00000",
    );
    let size = std::fs::metadata(&pkg_path).unwrap().len();

    // Simulate a queue that had prior activity: part ids start at 7, well
    // past anything a fresh file-server registry would hand out.
    let doc = serde_json::json!({
        "next_item": 5,
        "next_part": 8,
        "items": [{
            "id": 4,
            "parts": [{
                "id": 7,
                "path": pkg_path.to_string_lossy(),
                "file_name": "UP0001-CUSA09999_00-RESTOREDPKG00000.pkg",
                "size": size,
                "part_index": null,
                "pkg": null,
                "invalid_reason": null,
            }],
            "state": "Queued",
            "error": null,
            "task_id": null,
            "console_title": null,
            "progress": null,
            "attempts": 0,
            "added_at": 1,
            "updated_at": 1,
        }],
    });
    std::fs::write(
        data_dir.path().join("queue.json"),
        serde_json::to_string_pretty(&doc).unwrap(),
    )
    .unwrap();

    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::BrokenHex).await;
    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    // The restored item is already in that snapshot, queued.
    assert!(
        snap.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Queued),
        "restored queue must contain the pending item"
    );
    let item_id = snap
        .items
        .iter()
        .find(|i| i.state == ItemState::Queued)
        .unwrap()
        .id;

    handle.send(Command::Start);

    // With the bug this stalls forever (url_for misses, no log, no state
    // change); the fix must complete the install end to end.
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.id == item_id && i.state == ItemState::Completed)
        },
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    let item = snap.items.iter().find(|i| i.id == item_id).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(item.progress.unwrap().percent, 100.0);
    assert_eq!(mock.installs().len(), 1);

    // And the queue document now carries the server-scoped part id.
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(data_dir.path().join("queue.json")).unwrap();
    let q = rpi_ng_core::queue::Queue::from_json(&text).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        q.items()[0].parts[0].id,
        0,
        "part id must be the server registration"
    );
    handle.shutdown();
}

/// Pausing must stick: the console keeps reporting progress for a paused
/// task, and the poller must not overwrite the user's Paused state with
/// Transferring on every poll. Completing still applies.
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_pause_survives_progress_polls() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA11111_00-PAUSEPKG00000000.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA11111_00-PAUSEPKG00000000",
    );

    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::Strict).await;
    mock.state.lock().unwrap().step = 4096; // keep the transfer slow
    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(25),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![pkg_path],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| !s.items.is_empty(), Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Transferring),
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    let item_id = snap.items[0].id;

    // Pause mid-transfer.
    handle.send(Command::Pause);

    // Several progress polls arrive while paused — the state must stay Paused.
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.id == item_id && i.state == ItemState::Paused)
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    assert!(
        snap.items
            .iter()
            .any(|i| i.id == item_id && i.state == ItemState::Paused)
    );
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    let mut paused_stuck = false;
    for ev in handle.poll_events() {
        if let Event::Snapshot(s) = ev
            && s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.id == item_id && i.state == ItemState::Transferring)
        {
            paused_stuck = true;
        }
    }
    assert!(!paused_stuck, "poller must not overwrite the Paused state");

    // Resume finishes the install.
    handle.send(Command::Resume);
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.id == item_id && i.state == ItemState::Completed)
        },
        Duration::from_secs(30),
    )
    .await;
    handle.shutdown();
}

/// Disconnecting mid-transfer must not leave an orphaned Transferring item:
/// it becomes Unknown, and a reconnect re-attaches to the console task via
/// find_task and drives it to completion.
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_disconnect_marks_inflight_unknown_and_reattaches() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA22222_00-DISCONNECTPKG0.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA22222_00-DISCONNECTPKG0",
    );

    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::Strict).await;
    mock.state.lock().unwrap().step = 4096;
    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(25),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![pkg_path],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| !s.items.is_empty(), Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Transferring),
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    let item_id = snap.items[0].id;
    let task_id = snap.items[0].task_id.unwrap();

    // Teach the mock find_task for the real content id, like a real console
    // would report it after accepting the install.
    mock.state
        .lock()
        .unwrap()
        .by_content
        .insert("UP0001-CUSA22222_00-DISCONNECTPKG0".into(), task_id);

    // Drop the connection mid-flight.
    handle.send(Command::Disconnect);
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.id == item_id && i.state == ItemState::Unknown)
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    let item = snap.items.iter().find(|i| i.id == item_id).unwrap();
    assert!(item.error.is_some(), "unknown outcome needs a hint");

    // Reconnect: reconcile must re-attach to the console task and finish it.
    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.id == item_id && i.state == ItemState::Completed)
        },
        Duration::from_secs(30),
    )
    .await;
    handle.shutdown();
}

/// Connecting to a second console while a transfer is active used to leave
/// the engine in a mixed state: the poller kept watching the OLD console
/// (it holds the old client) while every remote control call and the status
/// chip pointed at the NEW one — and the file-server swap that follows a
/// connect cuts the old console's download anyway. A successful connect over
/// a live session must park in-flight items as Unknown (same semantics as an
/// explicit Disconnect), without stopping the old console's task.
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_connecting_elsewhere_parks_inflight_as_unknown() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA44444_00-SWITCHCONSOLE.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA44444_00-SWITCHCONSOLE",
    );

    let mock_a = MockConsole::start(Dialect::Strict).await;
    // Slow advance: the item must still be Transferring when we switch.
    mock_a.state.lock().unwrap().step = 4096;
    let mock_b = MockConsole::start(Dialect::Strict).await;

    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(25),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock_a.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![pkg_path],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| !s.items.is_empty(), Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Transferring),
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    let item_id = snap.items[0].id;

    // Connect to console B *without* an explicit Disconnect.
    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock_b.addr));
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            ) && s
                .items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.id == item_id && i.state == ItemState::Unknown)
        },
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    let item = snap.items.iter().find(|i| i.id == item_id).unwrap();
    assert!(
        item.task_id.is_some(),
        "the console task id is kept for a later re-attach"
    );
    assert!(
        item.error.is_some(),
        "an unknown outcome needs a user-facing hint"
    );

    // The old console's download was never cancelled (only unobserved), and
    // the new console saw no install traffic of its own.
    assert!(
        mock_a.stopped_tasks().is_empty(),
        "switching consoles must not stop the old console's task"
    );
    assert!(
        mock_b.installs().is_empty(),
        "the parked item must not be auto-resubmitted to the new console"
    );
    handle.shutdown();
}

/// A backend that accepts the install but answers without a task id (some
/// v3.x forks) must surface as Unknown — an untrackable outcome the user
/// verifies — not as Failed, which would claim the install never happened.
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_install_without_task_id_is_unknown_not_failed() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA33333_00-NOIDPKG000000000.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA33333_00-NOIDPKG000000000",
    );

    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::Strict).await;
    mock.state.lock().unwrap().omit_task_id = true;
    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![pkg_path],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| !s.items.is_empty(), Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| matches!(i.state, ItemState::Unknown | ItemState::Failed))
        },
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    let item = &snap.items[0];
    assert_eq!(
        item.state,
        ItemState::Unknown,
        "accepted-but-untrackable installs are unknown outcomes, not failures"
    );
    assert!(item.error.as_deref().unwrap().contains("task id"));
    // The console did accept the request.
    assert_eq!(mock.installs().len(), 1);
    handle.shutdown();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Engine end-to-end flow with the v2 API (NextGen-class mock)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Standard v2 harness: engine + v2 mock console + one valid fake PKG.
/// Both temp dirs are returned: dropping them deletes the pkg file
/// ("file vanished") and the queue persistence.
#[allow(clippy::type_complexity)]
async fn v2_engine_setup() -> (
    tempfile::TempDir,
    tempfile::TempDir,
    MockConsole,
    rpi_ng_core::engine::EngineHandle,
) {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA08888_00-MOCKGAMEV200000.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA08888_00-MOCKGAMEV200000",
    );
    let mock = MockConsole::start_v2().await;
    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));
    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    let target = snap.console.target.as_ref().expect("target");
    assert!(target.caps.api_v2, "engine must know the backend speaks v2");
    assert_eq!(
        target.backend.as_ref().expect("identity").name,
        "Remote PKG Installer NextGen"
    );
    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![pkg_path.clone()],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.parts.iter().any(|p| p.pkg.is_some()))
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    (data_dir, pkg_dir, mock, handle)
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_v2_end_to_end_flow_completes_with_evidence() {
    let (_data_dir, _pkg_dir, mock, handle) = v2_engine_setup().await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Completed),
        Duration::from_secs(30),
    )
    .await;
    let item = snap
        .items
        .iter()
        .find(|i| i.state == ItemState::Completed)
        .unwrap();
    let progress = item.progress.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(progress.total, 512 * 1024);
    assert_eq!(progress.percent, 100.0);
    assert_eq!(progress.raw_error, 0);
    assert!(item.task_id.unwrap() > 0);
    assert!(
        item.job_id.unwrap() > 0,
        "the console job id must be tracked"
    );
    assert_eq!(item.console_title.as_deref(), Some("Mock Game"));

    // The install went through the v2 endpoint with a `url` body.
    let v2_installs = mock.v2_installs();
    assert_eq!(v2_installs.len(), 1, "exactly one v2 install request");
    let url = v2_installs[0]["url"]
        .as_str()
        .expect("single-file url field");
    assert!(url.starts_with("http://"), "URL was {url}");

    // Progress came from the jobs list: the mock's job reached completed.
    let jobs = mock.jobs();
    assert_eq!(jobs.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(jobs[0].state, "completed");
    handle.shutdown();
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_v2_already_installed_completes_without_polling() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA09999_00-MOCKGAMEV2ALREADY.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA09999_00-MOCKGAMEV2ALREADY",
    );
    let mock = MockConsole::start_v2().await;
    mock.state.lock().unwrap().already_installed = true;
    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));
    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![pkg_path],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.parts.iter().any(|p| p.pkg.is_some()))
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    handle.send(Command::Start);

    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Completed),
        Duration::from_secs(30),
    )
    .await;
    let item = snap
        .items
        .iter()
        .find(|i| i.state == ItemState::Completed)
        .unwrap();
    assert!(
        item.task_id.is_none(),
        "no console task for already-installed"
    );
    assert!(item.job_id.is_some());
    handle.shutdown();
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_v2_pause_resume_uses_get_fallback() {
    let (_data_dir, _pkg_dir, mock, handle) = v2_engine_setup().await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);
    // Wait until the item is transferring, then pause.
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Transferring),
        Duration::from_secs(30),
    )
    .await;
    handle.send(Command::Pause);
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Paused),
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    assert!(
        mock.v2_op_posts("pause") >= 1,
        "pause must have gone POST-first (404) then GET"
    );

    handle.send(Command::Resume);
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Completed),
        Duration::from_secs(30),
    )
    .await;
    let item = snap
        .items
        .iter()
        .find(|i| i.state == ItemState::Completed)
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(item.progress.unwrap().percent, 100.0);
    handle.shutdown();
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_v2_disconnect_reconnect_reconciles_via_jobs() {
    let (_data_dir, _pkg_dir, mock, handle) = v2_engine_setup().await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);
    // Wait until transferring, then lose the connection.
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Transferring),
        Duration::from_secs(30),
    )
    .await;
    handle.send(Command::Disconnect);
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Unknown),
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    let item = &snap.items[0];
    let job_id = item.job_id.expect("job id survives the disconnect");

    // Reconnect: reconcile matches the item to the console's job by task id
    // (or job id), re-attaches the poller, and the install finishes. Waiting
    // directly for Completed proves the whole chain: match -> attach ->
    // poll -> console-side completion.
    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.job_id == Some(job_id) && i.state == ItemState::Completed)
        },
        Duration::from_secs(30),
    )
    .await;
    let item = snap
        .items
        .iter()
        .find(|i| i.job_id == Some(job_id))
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(item.state, ItemState::Completed);
    assert_eq!(item.progress.unwrap().percent, 100.0);
    // One v2 install, no resubmission after reconnect.
    assert_eq!(mock.v2_installs().len(), 1);
    handle.shutdown();
}

/// Regression test for the v0.0.1 settings bug: the engine must broadcast
/// every settings change (remembered consoles on connect, `SetSettings`
/// echo) so the UI's cached copy never goes stale.
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_broadcasts_settings_changes() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::BrokenHex).await;
    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    // 1. Connecting remembers the console and must broadcast the update.
    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    match wait_for_event(
        &handle,
        |ev| {
            matches!(
                ev,
                Event::SettingsChanged(s) if s.remembered.iter().any(|r| r.addr == mock.addr)
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await
    {
        Event::SettingsChanged(s) => assert_eq!(s.remembered[0].addr, mock.addr),
        _ => unreachable!("wait_for_event only returns matching events"),
    }

    // 2. SetSettings must echo the applied values back to the frontend.
    let changed = Settings {
        theme: ThemeMode::Dark,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    handle.send(Command::SetSettings(Box::new(changed.clone())));
    match wait_for_event(
        &handle,
        |ev| matches!(ev, Event::SettingsChanged(s) if *s == changed),
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await
    {
        Event::SettingsChanged(s) => assert_eq!(s, changed),
        _ => unreachable!("wait_for_event only returns matching events"),
    }

    handle.shutdown();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// v0.0.5 regressions: the submit window (request in flight, console has not
// answered yet)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Pressing Start again while the first install request is still in flight
/// used to submit the NEXT queued item too: `active` is only set when the
/// console answers, so the single-active-task invariant had a hole. The
/// second submit replaced the first item's poller and stranded it in
/// "Transferring" forever. The engine must serialize submits.
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_start_during_submit_window_does_not_double_submit() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let first = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA0D001_00-SUBMITRACEONE.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA0D001_00-SUBMITRACEONE",
    );
    let second = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA0D002_00-SUBMITRACETWO.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA0D002_00-SUBMITRACETWO",
    );

    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::BrokenHex).await;
    // Wide submit window: the install answer takes 400 ms.
    mock.state.lock().unwrap().install_delay_ms = 400;

    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![first, second],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| s.items.len() == 2, Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    // Two Starts back-to-back: the second lands while the first request is
    // still pending (deterministic — commands are queued, the answer is
    // delayed by the mock).
    handle.send(Command::Start);
    handle.send(Command::Start);

    // Both items must still complete — sequentially, not concurrently.
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().all(|i| i.state == ItemState::Completed),
        Duration::from_secs(30),
    )
    .await;
    assert_eq!(snap.items.len(), 2);
    assert_eq!(
        mock.installs().len(),
        2,
        "each package must be submitted exactly once"
    );
    assert_eq!(mock.tasks().len(), 2);
    handle.shutdown();
}

/// Cancelling while the install request is still in flight used to be a
/// silent no-op (the cancel path refused "active" items, and Submitting
/// counts as active) — the console's late answer then started the task
/// anyway. The cancel must stick, and the late answer's ghost task must be
/// stopped best-effort.
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_cancel_during_submit_window_is_honored() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSA0E001_00-SUBMITCANCEL.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSA0E001_00-SUBMITCANCEL",
    );

    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::BrokenHex).await;
    mock.state.lock().unwrap().install_delay_ms = 400;

    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![pkg_path],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| !s.items.is_empty(), Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Submitting),
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    let id = snap.items[0].id;

    // Cancel inside the submit window.
    handle.send(Command::Cancel(id));
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.id == id && i.state == ItemState::Cancelled)
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    // Let the delayed console answer land, then confirm: the item is still
    // Cancelled (not resurrected) and the ghost task was stopped.
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(700)).await;
    let mut latest = snap;
    for ev in handle.poll_events() {
        if let Event::Snapshot(s) = ev {
            latest = s;
        }
    }
    let item = latest.items.iter().find(|i| i.id == id).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(item.state, ItemState::Cancelled);
    assert_eq!(
        mock.installs().len(),
        1,
        "the request was already in flight when cancelled"
    );
    let stopped = mock.stopped_tasks();
    assert!(
        !stopped.is_empty(),
        "the late answer's task must be stopped best-effort, got {stopped:?}"
    );
    handle.shutdown();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Fourth audit round: fast-poll speed sampling, late-submit console scoping
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// v0.0.6 regression: the speed readout froze at 0 for poll intervals below
/// the 400 ms sampling threshold — the baseline timestamp was refreshed on
/// every poll, so consecutive samples never got far enough apart to be used.
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_speed_readout_works_at_fast_poll_intervals() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSASPEED_00-SPEEDTEST00.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSASPEED_00-SPEEDTEST00",
    );

    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::Strict).await;
    // A fixed 10 MiB task advanced 512 KiB per poll: ~20 polls of
    // Transferring, plenty of samples for the readout to converge.
    {
        let mut st = mock.state.lock().unwrap();
        st.fetch_urls = false;
        st.step = 512 * 1024;
    }
    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(250), // below the 0.4 s threshold
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![pkg_path],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| !s.items.is_empty(), Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items.iter().any(|i| {
                i.state == ItemState::Transferring
                    && i.progress.map(|p| p.speed_bps > 0).unwrap_or(false)
            })
        },
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    let item = snap
        .items
        .iter()
        .find(|i| i.state == ItemState::Transferring)
        .unwrap();
    let p = item.progress.unwrap();
    assert!(
        p.speed_bps >= 200_000,
        "a 250 ms poll cycle must still produce a speed readout, got {} B/s",
        p.speed_bps
    );
    handle.shutdown();
}

/// A delayed install answer that lands after a disconnect + reconnect must
/// keep the task id (for a later reconcile on that console) and must NOT stop
/// the console's task: disconnecting stops *observing*, not *controlling* —
/// only an explicit Cancel ever stops a console task. The v0.0.6 code fired
/// the stop through the new client on arrival, killing a download the design
/// means to re-attach to.
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_late_submit_after_disconnect_keeps_ids_and_task_alive() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSALATE_00-LATEANSWER.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSALATE_00-LATEANSWER",
    );

    let mock = MockConsole::start(Dialect::Strict).await;
    {
        let mut st = mock.state.lock().unwrap();
        // The prerequisite fetch runs after the delay in the mock; the local
        // file server is torn down by then, which would turn the late answer
        // into a (realistic) transfer error — this test wants the success
        // path, so the task is created without fetching.
        st.fetch_urls = false;
        st.install_delay_ms = 500;
    }

    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![pkg_path],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| !s.items.is_empty(), Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Submitting),
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    let id = snap.items[0].id;

    // Drop the connection while the request is in flight, then reconnect to
    // the SAME console before the answer lands.
    handle.send(Command::Disconnect);
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.id == id && i.state == ItemState::Unknown)
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    // Let the delayed answer land and fold in.
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(800)).await;
    let mut latest = None;
    for ev in handle.poll_events() {
        if let Event::Snapshot(s) = ev {
            latest = Some(s);
        }
    }
    let latest = latest.expect("a snapshot follows the late answer");
    let item = latest.items.iter().find(|i| i.id == id).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        item.state,
        ItemState::Unknown,
        "a late answer must not resurrect a parked item"
    );
    assert!(
        item.task_id.is_some(),
        "the id is kept for a later re-attach"
    );
    assert!(
        item.task_owner.is_some_and(|a| a == mock.addr),
        "the id is attributed to the console it belongs to"
    );
    assert!(
        mock.stopped_tasks().is_empty(),
        "disconnecting must not stop the console's task, got {:?}",
        mock.stopped_tasks()
    );
    handle.shutdown();
}

/// Cancelling during the submit window and THEN switching consoles: the
/// best-effort stop of the ghost task must go through the console the
/// request was actually sent to — the current client points at the new
// console, and firing the old task id there could stop an unrelated task
/// (ids are console-scoped and collide).
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_cancel_then_console_switch_stops_only_the_owner_console() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSASWIT_00-SWITCHSTOP.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSASWIT_00-SWITCHSTOP",
    );

    let mock_a = MockConsole::start(Dialect::Strict).await;
    {
        // See the late-answer test: the mock's prerequisite fetch would run
        // after the delay against a file server that is already gone; this
        // test needs the task to be created so the late stop has a target.
        let mut st = mock_a.state.lock().unwrap();
        st.fetch_urls = false;
        st.install_delay_ms = 500;
    }
    let mock_b = MockConsole::start(Dialect::Strict).await;

    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(30),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock_a.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![pkg_path],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| !s.items.is_empty(), Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Submitting),
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    let id = snap.items[0].id;

    // Cancel inside the submit window…
    handle.send(Command::Cancel(id));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.id == id && i.state == ItemState::Cancelled)
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    // …then connect to console B before the delayed answer lands.
    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock_b.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.console.target.as_ref().map(|t| t.addr) == Some(mock_b.addr),
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    // Let A's delayed answer land and fold in.
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(800)).await;
    for ev in handle.poll_events() {
        let _ = ev;
    }

    // The ghost-task cleanup went to the console that owns the task…
    let stopped_a = mock_a.stopped_tasks();
    assert!(
        !stopped_a.is_empty(),
        "the late answer's task must still be stopped on the console it was sent to"
    );
    // …and the new console never saw a stop or install aimed at it.
    assert!(
        mock_b.stopped_tasks().is_empty(),
        "the new console must not receive the old task id, got {:?}",
        mock_b.stopped_tasks()
    );
    assert!(
        mock_b.installs().is_empty(),
        "nothing must be auto-submitted to the new console"
    );
    handle.shutdown();
}

/// Reconcile must not adopt a foreign console's task id: ids are
/// console-scoped and collide across machines (BGFT counters start low on
/// every console). A queue item parked as Unknown with console A's task id
/// must not silently attach to console B's *unrelated* task with the same
/// numeric id — content ids remain a valid cross-console match.
#[tokio::test]
async fn engine_reconcile_does_not_adopt_a_foreign_consoles_task_id() {
    let data_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();
    let pkg_path = build_fake_pkg(
        pkg_dir.path(),
        "UP0001-CUSAFORE_00-FOREIGNID0.pkg",
        "UP0001-CUSAFORE_00-FOREIGNID0",
    );

    let mock_a = MockConsole::start(Dialect::Strict).await;
    mock_a.state.lock().unwrap().step = 4096; // still Transferring at the switch
    let mock_b = MockConsole::start(Dialect::Strict).await;

    let settings = Settings {
        poll_interval: Duration::from_millis(25),
        discovery_enabled: false,
        ..Settings::default()
    };
    let handle = rpi_ng_core::engine::spawn_with(settings, Some(data_dir.path().to_path_buf()));

    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock_a.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            matches!(
                s.console.status,
                rpi_ng_core::model::ConsoleStatus::Connected
            )
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    handle.send(Command::AddPaths {
        paths: vec![pkg_path],
        recursive: false,
    });
    wait_for_snapshot(&handle, |s| !s.items.is_empty(), Duration::from_secs(10)).await;

    handle.send(Command::Start);
    let snap = wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.items.iter().any(|i| i.state == ItemState::Transferring),
        Duration::from_secs(20),
    )
    .await;
    let id = snap.items[0].id;
    let task_id = snap.items[0].task_id.unwrap();

    // Console B independently runs its own task with the SAME numeric id,
    // for a different package.
    mock_b.state.lock().unwrap().tasks.insert(
        task_id,
        MockTask {
            total: 10 * 1024 * 1024,
            transferred: 1024 * 1024,
            local_copy: 0,
            error: 0,
        },
    );

    // Park the item as Unknown, then connect to console B.
    handle.send(Command::Disconnect);
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| {
            s.items
                .iter()
                .any(|i| i.id == id && i.state == ItemState::Unknown)
        },
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;
    handle.send(Command::Connect(mock_b.addr));
    wait_for_snapshot(
        &handle,
        |s| s.console.target.as_ref().map(|t| t.addr) == Some(mock_b.addr),
        Duration::from_secs(10),
    )
    .await;

    // Give reconcile room to (wrongly) attach, then inspect.
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    let mut latest = None;
    for ev in handle.poll_events() {
        if let Event::Snapshot(s) = ev {
            latest = Some(s);
        }
    }
    let latest = latest.expect("a snapshot follows the connect");
    let item = latest.items.iter().find(|i| i.id == id).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        item.state,
        ItemState::Unknown,
        "a foreign console's task id must not be adopted (B's task {} is another package)",
        task_id
    );
    assert!(
        item.error
            .as_deref()
            .is_some_and(|e| e.contains("does not report")),
        "the item keeps the verify-on-console hint, got {:?}",
        item.error
    );
    handle.shutdown();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Fifth audit round — discovery re-validates remembered hosts on their port
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// A remembered console living on a custom port (manual entry, e.g.
/// `192.168.1.42:12801`) must be re-verifiable by discovery on that port.
/// `verify_addr` used to probe only the two standard ports, so such a
/// console silently failed re-validation despite being reachable and
/// connectable — the mock binds an ephemeral port, which is guaranteed to be
/// non-standard, making the case deterministic without a fixed port.
#[tokio::test]
async fn discovery_verifies_remembered_hosts_on_their_own_port() {
    let console = MockConsole::start(Dialect::Strict).await;
    let candidate = rpi_ng_core::discovery::verify_addr(
        console.addr,
        rpi_ng_core::model::DiscoverySource::Remembered,
    )
    .await
    .expect("a console on a non-standard port must verify on that port");

    assert_eq!(candidate.addr, console.addr);
    assert_eq!(
        candidate.source,
        rpi_ng_core::model::DiscoverySource::Remembered
    );
}

```

`crates/rpi-ng-core/tests/update_test.rs`:

```rs
//! Update-checker tests against a mock of the GitHub releases API.
//!
//! The mock reproduces the response shapes GitHub actually sends (including
//! the 404 "Not Found" body when a repo has no releases and the 403 rate-limit
//! body), so the client's classification logic is exercised against realistic
//! inputs without touching the network.

use std::{net::SocketAddr, time::Duration};

use axum::{Json, Router, http::StatusCode, response::IntoResponse, routing::get};
use serde_json::json;

use rpi_ng_core::update::{self, UpdateError};

async fn mock_api() -> SocketAddr {
    let app = Router::new()
        .route(
            "/newer",
            get(|| async {
                Json(json!({
                    "tag_name": "v9.9.9",
                    "html_url": "https://example.com/rpi-ng/tag/v9.9.9"
                }))
            }),
        )
        .route(
            "/same",
            get(|| async {
                Json(json!({
                    "tag_name": "v1.2.3",
                    "html_url": "https://example.com/rpi-ng/tag/v1.2.3"
                }))
            }),
        )
        .route(
            "/older",
            get(|| async {
                Json(json!({
                    "tag_name": "v0.0.1",
                    "html_url": "https://example.com/rpi-ng/tag/v0.0.1"
                }))
            }),
        )
        .route("/no-release", get(|| async { not_found() }))
        .route(
            "/rate-limit",
            get(|| async {
                (
                    StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,
                    Json(json!({"message": "API rate limit exceeded for 1.2.3.4."})),
                )
            }),
        )
        .route("/garbage", get(|| async { "this is not json" }))
        .route(
            "/empty-tag",
            get(|| async { Json(json!({"tag_name": "", "html_url": "https://x"})) }),
        )
        .route(
            "/no-url",
            get(|| async { Json(json!({"tag_name": "v1.2.4"})) }),
        );

    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let addr = listener.local_addr().unwrap();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();
    });
    addr
}

fn not_found() -> impl IntoResponse {
    (
        StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,
        Json(json!({"message": "Not Found", "documentation_url": "https://docs.github.com"})),
    )
}

fn client() -> reqwest::Client {
    reqwest::Client::builder()
        .user_agent("rpi-ng-test")
        .timeout(Duration::from_secs(10))
        .build()
        .unwrap()
}

async fn check(path: &str, current: &str) -> Result<Option<update::UpdateInfo>, UpdateError> {
    let addr = mock_api().await;
    let url = format!("http://{addr}{path}");
    update::check_latest(&client(), &url, current).await
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn newer_release_is_reported() {
    let found = check("/newer", "1.2.3")
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .expect("update expected");
    assert_eq!(found.version, "9.9.9");
    assert_eq!(found.tag, "v9.9.9");
    assert_eq!(found.url, "https://example.com/rpi-ng/tag/v9.9.9");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn same_release_reports_nothing() {
    assert!(check("/same", "1.2.3").await.unwrap().is_none());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn older_release_reports_nothing() {
    assert!(check("/older", "1.2.3").await.unwrap().is_none());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn repo_without_releases_reports_nothing() {
    // GitHub answers 404 here; that is "no news", not an error.
    assert!(check("/no-release", "1.2.3").await.unwrap().is_none());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn rate_limit_is_classified() {
    let err = check("/rate-limit", "1.2.3").await.unwrap_err();
    assert!(matches!(err, UpdateError::RateLimited), "got {err:?}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn malformed_body_is_an_error() {
    let err = check("/garbage", "1.2.3").await.unwrap_err();
    assert!(matches!(err, UpdateError::Malformed(_)), "got {err:?}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn empty_tag_is_malformed() {
    let err = check("/empty-tag", "1.2.3").await.unwrap_err();
    assert!(matches!(err, UpdateError::Malformed(_)), "got {err:?}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn missing_html_url_falls_back_to_the_releases_page() {
    let found = check("/no-url", "1.2.3")
        .await
        .unwrap()
        .expect("update expected");
    assert_eq!(found.tag, "v1.2.4");
    assert_eq!(found.url, format!("{}/tag/v1.2.4", update::RELEASES_PAGE));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn unreachable_api_is_a_network_error() {
    // Bind then drop a listener to get a refused port.
    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind(SocketAddr::from(([127, 0, 0, 1], 0)))
        .await
        .unwrap();
    let addr = listener.local_addr().unwrap();
    drop(listener);
    let err = update::check_latest(&client(), &format!("http://{addr}/x"), "1.2.3")
        .await
        .unwrap_err();
    assert!(matches!(err, UpdateError::Network(_)), "got {err:?}");
}
```
